	type = country

		trigger = {
			NOT = { zz_ef_has_currency_identifier = yes }
		}

		localization_key = spe_uni_good
	}

currency_symbol = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	# the sign of the codes that have one, else the code (Д.R8в.20)
	#no monetary system
	text = {
		trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system }
		localization_key = zz_ef_sym_none
	}
	#DEM
	text = {
		trigger = {
			var:zz_ef_cur_noun ?= flag:mark
			OR = {
				AND = { NOT = { zz_ef_mu_follower = yes } c:GER ?= this }
				AND = { has_variable = zz_ef_mu_head c:GER ?= var:zz_ef_mu_head }
			}
		}
		localization_key = zz_ef_sym_code_DEM
	}
	#ESP
	text = {
		trigger = {
			var:zz_ef_cur_noun ?= flag:peseta
			OR = {
				AND = { NOT = { zz_ef_mu_follower = yes } c:SPA ?= this }
				AND = { has_variable = zz_ef_mu_head c:SPA ?= var:zz_ef_mu_head }
			}
		}
		localization_key = zz_ef_sym_code_ESP
	}

		trigger = {
			var:zz_ef_cur_noun ?= flag:franc

monetary_system = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
			}
		}

		localization_key = gold_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
		}

		localization_key = bimetallism_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		}

		localization_key = silver_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_external_exchange_standard
		}

		localization_key = bimetallism_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
			}
		}

		localization_key = spe_uni_good
	}
}
monetary_system_name = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
		}

		localization_key = gold_name
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
		}

		localization_key = bimetallism_name
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		}

		localization_key = silver_name
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_external_exchange_standard
		}

		localization_key = bimetallism_name
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
			}
		}

		localization_key = spe_uni
	}
}

monetary_system_partiel = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
				has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
				has_law = law_type:law_external_exchange_standard
			}
		}

		localization_key = gold_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		}

		localization_key = silver_good
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
			}
		}

		localization_key = spe_uni_good
	}
}

			trigger = { stockpiling_dinar_added_per_month > 0 }

		}

			trigger = { stockpiling_dinar_added_per_month < 0 }
			localization_key = red_arow_bellow
		}
		text = {
			trigger = { stockpiling_dinar_added_per_month = 0 }
			localization_key = yellow_arrow

subject_type_icon = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = {
            is_subject_type = subject_type_protectorate
		}

		localization_key = protectorate_texture
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			is_subject_type = subject_type_dominion
		}

		}

		}

############################################################################################################################################################


inflation_genrated = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = {
			money_supply_state_ratio > 0.1
		}

		localization_key = yes
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			money_supply_state_ratio < 0.1
		}

		localization_key = no
	}
}

#crisis

############################################################################################################################################################
############################################################################################################################################################
############################################################################################################################################################
############################################################################################################################################################

#currency_crisis
currency_crisis_country = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = { 
			has_modifier = currency_crisis_country 
		}

		localization_key = active
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			not = { 
				has_modifier = currency_crisis_country
			}
			var:currency_crisis = 1
		}

		localization_key = stabilizing
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			not = { 
				has_modifier = currency_crisis_country
			}
			var:currency_crisis = 0
		}

		localization_key = inactive
	}
}
#central_bank_bankruptcy
central_bank_bankruptcy_country = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = { 
			has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country 
		}

		localization_key = active
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			not = { 
				has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
			}
			var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 1
		}

		localization_key = critical
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			not = { 
				has_modifier = central_bank_bankruptcy_country
			}
			var:central_bank_bankruptcy = 0
		}

		localization_key = inactive
	}
}

economic_instability = {
	type = country
	log_loc_errors = no

	text = {
		trigger = { 
			var:economic_instability_count >=1
		}

		localization_key = active
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			var:economic_instability_count = 0
		}

		localization_key = inactive
	}
